```
U = np.zeros((m,n))
for idx,k in enumerate(K):
    lo = max(0, k-(n-1))
    hi = min(n-1, k)
    length = hi-lo+1
    U[idx, :length] = a[hi:lo-1:-1] if length>0 else 0
```
For k<n, hi=k, lo=0, row starts a[k],a[k-1]... length k+1.
For k>=n-1, lo=k-n+1, hi=n-1, length n-1-lo, row starts a[n-1],a[n-2]... length. Good.

```
A = G + lam*I
# If b norm zero? no.
AA = np.zeros((m+1,m+1)); AA[:m,:m]=A; AA[:m,m]=b; AA[m,:m]=b; AA[m,m]=0
rr = np.zeros(m+1); rr[:m]=r
sol = np.linalg.solve(AA, rr) or lstsq if singular
x=sol[:m]
d=U.T @ x
d -= d.mean()
```

Pseudo:
```
def _active_flatten_loop(a, deadline, min_time_remaining, max_iters=200, m_rows=160, eta_start=0.1, eta_end=0.8):
    best_a = normalize(a); best_sc=score(best_a)
    n=len(best_a); eta=eta_start
    lam=1e-6
    no_improve=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic()+min_time_remaining>deadline or no_improve>... break
       a=best_a
       c=conv(a,a); cmax=c.max(); mean=c.mean()
       target = cmax - eta*(cmax-mean)  # if target<=0 target=mean
       # if target < mean? eta>1
       # Select rows: top m by abs(c-target)
       if m_rows >= L: K=np.arange(L) else:
          abs_e=np.abs(c-target)
          # maybe ensure central included; use argpartition
          K=np.argpartition(-abs_e, m_rows-1)[:m_rows]
       a_new, sc_new = _active_step(a,c,K,target,lam,m_rows,n)
       if sc_new < best_sc - 1e-13:
           best_a=a_new; best_sc=sc_new; no_improve=0; eta = min(eta_end, eta*1.2)
       else:
           no_improve+=1
           # try smaller lam or eta
           if no_improve==1: lam*=10
           elif no_improve==2: target = cmax - 0.5*(cmax-mean); lam*=0.1? 
           else: break or use L-BFGS
    return best_a,best_sc
```
Need `_active_step` compute U, solve, line search. It needs c? Can recompute? We pass c to avoid conv. But after a_try need score -> conv. Use _score.

Details `_active_step`:
```
def _active_step(a, c, K, target, lam, gamma=1.0):
    n=len(a); m=len(K)
    U=np.zeros((m,n))
    for idx,k in enumerate(K):
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k); length=hi-lo+1
       if length>0:
          U[idx,:length]=a[hi:lo-1:-1]
    e = c[K]-target
    r = -0.5*gamma*e
    G=U@U.T
    A=G+lam*np.eye(m)
    b=U.sum(axis=1)
    # If G near zero (rows tiny), add larger lam
    A_norm = np.max(np.abs(G)) + 1.0
    lam_eff = lam*A_norm + 1e-12
    A += ...? Use A=G + (lam*A_norm+1e-12)I
    # Solve augmented
    AA=np.zeros((m+1,m+1)); ...
    try sol=np.linalg.solve(AA,rr)
    except: sol=np.linalg.lstsq(AA,rr,rcond=None)[0]
    x=sol[:m]
    d=U.T@x
    d -= d.mean()
    # scale d to reasonable norm
    d_norm=np.linalg.norm(d)
    if d_norm==0: return a,None
    # We want step size such that relative change not huge. Scale d initially by target? The r had scale e ~c ~1/n. d norm likely? We'll use line search over tablo.
    # Limit max component: max_change = 0.5* max(a)? Better use min positive a.
    # Choose alpha candidates: start 1.0 but scale d so max |d| <= 0.1 * max(a)?
    max_step_allowed = 0.1 * np.max(a)  # a values ~1/n, so 0.006/n
    scale = min(1.0, max_step_allowed / (np.max(np.abs(d))+1e-300))
    d *= scale
    best_a=a; best_sc=score(a)
    for alpha in [1,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.01]:
       a_try = a + alpha*d
       # clip
       a_try[a_try<0]=0
       s=a_try.sum()
       if s<1e-12: continue
       a_try/=s
       sc=_score(a_try)
       if sc<best_sc:
          return a_try, sc
    # If no improvement with this direction, try small positive/negative? d direction from LS should be descent; if not, could flip? Flipping would raise target? Maybe if sign wrong. We can try -d if symmetric? But flipping may increase max. Try small both? We can include -alpha for first few.
    for alpha in [0.1,0.05,0.01]:
       a_try = a - alpha*d; ... if improves return
    return a, None
```

Now `_run_lbfgs_seq` details:
```
def _run_lbfgs_seq(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining, max_betas=20, aggressive=False, label=''):
    global _CUR_N, _CUR_BETA
    a = normalize(a_init)
    n=len(a)
    _CUR_N=n
    c=_full_convolve(a,a); maxc=c.max()
    if maxc <=0: return a
    sorted_c=np.sort(c)[::-1]
    idx90=int(0.9*len(c))
    gap=maxc - sorted_c[min(idx90,len(c)-1)]
    base=max(gap*0.5, maxc*0.01)
    min_beta=max(maxc*1e-8 if aggressive else maxc*1e-6, 1e-30)
    # ratios
    ratios=[1.0,0.7,0.5,0.35,0.25,0.175,0.125,0.09,0.06,0.04,0.025,0.015,0.008,0.004,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.00025,0.000125,0.0000625]
    betas=[]
    for r in ratios:
       b=base*r
       if b<min_beta: b=min_beta
       if len(betas)==0 or b < betas[-1]*0.98: betas.append(b)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z=z-z.mean()
    for beta in betas:
       if time.monotonic()+min_time_remaining>deadline: break
       remaining=deadline-min_time_remaining-time.monotonic()
       if remaining<5: break
       _CUR_BETA=float(beta)
       est=0.004*max(1.0,n/8192.0)
       maxiter=min(5000, max(30, int(remaining/(est*20)))) #? Need not too cap.
       try:
          res=minimize(_obj_scipy, z, jac=_grad_scipy, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-18,'gtol':1e-14,'maxcor':60,'maxls':100})
          z_new=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       except Exception:
          break
       a_new=_softmax_centered(z_new)
       sc=_score(a_new)
       _try_update(a_new, sc, f'{label}_b{beta:.2e}', time.monotonic()-_START)
       if a_new is better than current best? We can keep local best_a.
       # update local best if sc<best local
       a=a_new; z=z_new - z_new.mean()
    return a
```

Smooth objective and gradient implementation:
```
def _softmax_centered(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size==0: return np.array([])
    z=z-np.mean(z)
    m=np.max(z)
    e=np.exp(np.clip(z-m, -700, 700))
    s=e.sum()
    if s<1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.full(z.shape,1.0/len(z))
    return e/s

def _smoobj_scipy(z):
    beta=_CUR_BETA; n=_CUR_N
    a=_softmax_centered(z)
    c=_full_convolve(a,a)
    L=c.size
    m=float(c.max())
    u=(c-m)/beta
    # stable logsum
    u_max=np.max(u) # will be 0? since m max, u<=0, u_max=0 if beta finite
    s=np.sum(np.exp(np.clip(u, -700, 0.0)))
    if s<1e-300 or not finite: logsum=0.0
    else: logsum=np.log(s)
    val=m + beta*(logsum - np.log(L))
    return float(2.0*n*val)

def _smograd_scipy(z):
    beta=_CUR_BETA; n=_CUR_N
    a=_softmax_centered(z)
    c=_full_convolve(a,a)
    L=c.size
    m=float(c.max())
    u=(c-m)/beta
    u_clip=np.clip(u, -700, 0.0)
    e=np.exp(u_clip)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<1e-300: w=np.zeros(L); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
    else: w=e/s
    a_rev=a[::-1]
    # B = conv(a_rev, w)[n-1:2n-1]
    hw=_full_convolve(a_rev, w)
    B=hw[n-1:2*n-1]
    W=float(np.dot(w,c))
    grad=4.0*n*a*(B - W)
    grad = grad - grad.mean()
    return grad
```
Check B slice with _full_convolve(a_rev,w). _full_convolve returns length 3n-2? a_rev n, w L=2n-1 => length 3n-2. Slice n-1:2n-1 length n. As tested.
If beta extremely small, e for max maybe 1, others 0, s=1, w one-hot. B-W? At optimum may small. If max multiple, only one due argmax if underflow. Fine.

Need `_full_convolve` output length correct and no negative due roundoff. Use rfft. We'll set:
```
def _full_convolve(a,b):
    na,nb=len(a),len(b); out=na+nb-1
    N=1<<(out-1).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,N)[:out]
```
Maybe for real input, irfft returns length N/2+1? Actually np.fft.irfft(a,N) returns length N. Good.

For a_rev,w where w length 2n-1, output length 3n-2. N next pow2. Fine.

Score:
```
def _score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a); n=len(a); s=float(a.sum())
    if s<1e-300 or not finite: return np.inf
    c=_full_convolve(a,a)
    if len(c)==0: return np.inf
    maxc=float(np.max(c))
    if not finite: return np.inf
    return 2.0*n*maxc/(s*s)
```
If a values not normalized, okay.

Normalize:
```
def _normalize(a):
    a=np.clip(np.asarray(a,dtype=float),0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-30: return a
    return a/s
```

Try update:
```
def _try_update(seq, score, label):
    global _BEST_SEQ, _BEST_SCORE
    if seq is None: return
    arr=np.asarray(seq,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size==0: return
    arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if s<0.01: return
    arr=arr/s
    sc=_score(arr)
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return
    if sc < _BEST_SCORE:
       _BEST_SEQ=arr.copy(); _BEST_SCORE=float(sc)
       print(f"NEW BEST {sc:.10f} n={len(arr)} {label} t={time.monotonic()-_PROPOSE_START:.1f}")
```
Need `_PROPOSE_START` global set.

But recomputing score for every `_try_update` may be costly if called often. We'll call only at phases and maybe within L-BFGS per beta (few). Good.

`_active_step` should use `_score` too. It may call multiple times.

Now active flatten loop details. Need global `RNG`? We can pass random number generator as argument. Top-level functions can take rng.

```
def _select_active_rows(c, target, m_rows, rng):
    L=len(c)
    if m_rows >= L: return np.arange(L)
    # deviations weighted by feasibility? We don't know b yet. Use abs(c-target)
    dev=np.abs(c-target)
    # We can avoid picking all edges with zero? Add small random
    # Use argpartition
    idx=np.argpartition(-dev, m_rows-1)[:m_rows]
    return idx
```
But we want maybe include highest c: top `m_high` by c, plus random. Let's do:
```
def _select_rows(c,target,m_rows,rng,frac_high=0.6):
    L=len(c)
    if m_rows>=L: return np.arange(L)
    m_high=max(1,int(m_rows*frac_high))
    m_rand=m_rows-m_high
    high_idx=np.argpartition(-c, m_high-1)[:m_high]
    rand_idx=rng.choice(L, size=m_rand, replace=False)
    # combine unique, maybe if rand duplicates high
    idx=np.unique(np.concatenate([high_idx, rand_idx]))
    if len(idx)>m_rows: idx=idx[:m_rows]
    return idx
```
This focuses on high plus random. Random may include lows. Good.

Active step with weighting by b:
```
def _active_l_step(a, target, m_rows, lam, rng, gamma=1.0):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1; c=_full_convolve(a,a)
    rows=_select_rows(c,target,m_rows,rng)
    m=len(rows)
    U=np.zeros((m,n), dtype=np.float64)
    for ii,k in enumerate(rows):
       k=int(k)
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       length=hi-lo+1
       if length>0:
          U[ii,:length]=a[hi:lo-1:-1]
    e=c[rows]-target
    b=U.sum(axis=1)
    bpos=b[b>0]
    bmean=float(bpos.mean()) if bpos.size else 1.0
    if bmean<1e-300: bmean=1.0
    scale=np.clip(b/bmean, 0.0, 1.0) # downweight small b
    r=-0.5*gamma*e*scale
    G=U@U.T
    gmax=float(np.max(np.abs(G))) if G.size else 0.0
    lam_eff=lam*gmax + 1e-12
    A=G + lam_eff*np.eye(m)
    # solve augmented
    AA=np.zeros((m+1,m+1)); AA[:m,:m]=A; AA[:m,m]=b; AA[m,:m]=b
    rr=np.zeros(m+1); rr[:m]=r
    try: sol=np.linalg.solve(AA, rr)
    except: sol=np.linalg.lstsq(AA, rr, rcond=None)[0]
    x=sol[:m]
    d=U.T @ x
    d -= d.mean()
    d_norm=np.linalg.norm(d)
    if d_norm<1e-300: return None
    # scale direction to typical a scale
    a_max=float(np.max(a))
    d_max=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if d_max>1e-300:
       target_max=0.2*a_max  # allow 20% of a value
       d *= min(1.0, target_max/d_max)
    # line search
    best_a=a; best_sc=_score(a)
    # Try alphas
    alphas=[1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02]
    for alpha in alphas:
       a_try=a+alpha*d
       if np.any(a_try<0): a_try=np.maximum(a_try,0)
       s=a_try.sum()
       if s<1e-300: continue
       a_try=a_try/s
       sc=_score(a_try)
       if sc<best_sc:
          return a_try, sc
    # If no, try negative small? maybe if sign wrong. Use alpha 0.05 negative
    for alpha in [0.1,0.05,0.02]:
       a_try=a-alpha*d; clip... if sc<best return
    return None
```
Note `target_max=0.2*a_max` maybe too small; a values can vary. Use relative to a norm? If a_max ~1/n, step 0.02/n. Good.
Line search calls `_score` each with conv. Could be expensive but okay.

Active loop:
```
def _active_flatten_loop(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining, max_iters=100, m_rows=160, eta_start=0.2, eta_end=0.8):
    best_a=_normalize(a_init); best_sc=_score(best_a)
    n=len(best_a); eta=eta_start; lam=1e-5
    stall=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic()+min_time_remaining>deadline: break
       if stall>6: break
       c=_full_convolve(best_a,best_a); cmax=float(c.max()); mean=float(c.mean())
       if cmax<=mean: break
       target=cmax - eta*(cmax-mean)
       if target<mean*0.25: target=mean*0.25
       res=_active_l_step(best_a,target,m_rows,lam,rng)
       if res is None:
          stall+=1
          if stall<=2: lam*=5
          elif stall==3: eta=max(0.1,eta*0.7); lam=1e-5
          else: break
          continue
       a_new, sc_new=res
       if sc_new < best_sc - 1e-13:
          best_a=a_new; best_sc=sc_new; stall=0
          eta=min(eta_end, eta*1.15)
          # maybe lam=min(0.1,lam*0.8) to be less regularized
       else:
          # try smaller target (less aggressive) maybe
          stall+=1
       _try_update(best_a,best_sc,'active')
    return best_a,best_sc
```
But `_active_l_step` recomputes c; we compute c outside too. We can modify to accept c to avoid. Let's make `_active_l_step(a, c, target, m_rows, lam, rng)`. Then loop computes c. It returns new a and score. It uses c for row. Good.

```
def _active_l_step(a, c, target, m_rows, lam, rng):
   ... rows from c ...
```
Line search needs score; use `_score` which recomputes.

We can reduce line search evaluations by using `sc=_score(a_try)`; if no improvement. Accept if sc < best_sc*(1-1e-12). Good.

def _mirror_polish(a_init, deadline, min_time, max_steps=500, beta_frac=0.005):
    a=...; best=score
    z=log(a)-mean
    for i:
       c=conv; maxc; beta=maxc*beta_frac
       u=(c-maxc)/beta; w=exp(clip(u))/sum; add floor? w += 1e-6? renorm
       B=conv(a_rev,w); W=dot
       d=a*(W-B) # negative gradient direction (sincegrad= a(B-W))
       d -=? in log space d_z = a*(W-B), already sum d_z? sum a_i(W-B_i)=W - sum a B = W - W =0 (because sum a B = W). Nice! So z change preserves normalization approximately (softmax of centered? z update with sum zero keeps mean). a_try=softmax(z+eta*d). line search.
```
Since d_z sum zero, no scaling. Step size adaptive. Backtracking: eta start 0.5, reduce. Compute score. This is like gradient descent in softmax. Could be useful as final. But L-BFGS similar. We can include if time.

Need no closure. Top-level.

Maybe better than L-BFGS at very small beta? It can use floor weights for direction while line search uses score. Let's implement `_mirror_polish` with floor. It may escape symmetric if floor/d random.

```
def _mirror_polish(a_init, deadline, min_time, max_steps=200, beta_frac=0.01, floor=1e-5):
   best_a=normalize; best_sc=score; z=log(a)-mean; eta=1.0
   for step:
     if deadline break
     c=conv best; maxc; beta=maxc*beta_frac
     u=(c-maxc)/beta
     e=exp(clip(u)); s=e.sum(); w=e/s
     w += floor*np.ones(L); w/=w.sum()
     B=conv(a_rev,w)[...]; W=dot
     d=a*(W-B)  # direction to increase z
     # d sum ~0; clip if large
     d_norm=np.linalg.norm(d); if small break
     # scale d to step: a relative change about eta*d; limit max |eta*d/a|? 
     eta_try=1.0
     improved=False
     for stays in range(20):
        z_trial=z + eta_try*d
        a_trial=softmax(z_trial)
        sc=score(a_trial)
        if sc<best_sc:
           best_a=a_trial; best_sc=sc; z=z_trial; eta=min(1.5, eta*1.1); improved=True; break
        eta_try*=0.5
        if eta_try<1e-6: break
     if not improved:
        # maybe random perturb and continue
        stall++...
   return best
```
This may be slower but okay. Use after L-BFGS.

But `softmax(z+ηd)` if d sum not exactly zero, re-normalizes anyway. Good.

Can use this as a general optimizer from random restarts.

Let's write smooth functions:
```
def _softmax_centered(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=z.size
    if n==0: return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    z=z-np.mean(z)
    m=np.max(z)
    e=np.exp(np.clip(z-m, -700.0, 700.0))
    s=float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0/n, dtype=np.float64)
    a=e/s
    # maybe renormalize
    return a
```

`_opt_objective`:
```
def _opt_objective(z):
    n=_CUR_N; beta=_CUR_BETA
    if n<=0: return 0.0
    a=_softmax_centered(z)
    c=_full_convolve(a,a)
    L=c.size
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m): return 1e100
    if beta <= 0: return 2.0*n*m
    u=(c-m)/beta
    # u max 0
    u=np.clip(u, -700.0, 0.0)
    s=float(np.exp(u).sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
       logsum=0.0
    else:
       logsum=float(np.log(s))
    val=m + beta*(logsum - np.log(float(L)))
    return float(2.0*n*val)
```
If beta very small, u for nonmax < -700 -> exp0, s maybe number of max. logsum=log(k). val=m+beta(logk-lnL). Good.

`_opt_gradient`:
```
def _opt_gradient(z):
   n=_CUR_N; beta=_CUR_BETA
   a=_softmax_centered(z)
   c=_full_convolve(a,a); L=c.size; m=max; if beta<=0: w one-hot at argmax? For beta<=0 return subgradient top: w zeros, w[argmax]=1.
   else:
     u=(c-m)/beta; u=clip; e=exp; s=sum; if s bad: one-hot
     w=e/s
   B=_full_convolve(a[::-1], w)[n-1:2*n-1]
   W=float(np.dot(w,c))
   grad=4.0*float(n)*a*(B-W)
   grad=grad-grad.mean()
   if not all finite: return zeros
   return grad
```
For beta<=0, maybe not used. Ensure B length n: if `hw` length maybe 3n-2. Slice. If n=1, slice? n=1, L=1, hw length1, slice n-1:2n-1 =0:2? length1? For n=1 okay? Parent n large. But safe: `end=min(len(hw),2*n-1)` etc. But for n=1: 2n-2=0, want index0. `n-1=0`, `2*n-1=1`, slice0:1 ok.

```
def _gradient_polish(a_init, deadline, min_time_remaining, max_steps=200, beta_frac=0.005, floor=1e-6):
   a=_normalize(a_init); best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a); n=len(a); L=2n-1
   z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z=z-z.mean()
   eta=1.0; stall=0
   for it in range(max_steps):
      if time.monotonic()+min_time_remaining>deadline: break
      c=_full_convolve(a,a); m=max
      if m<=0: break
      beta=max(m*beta_frac, 1e-12)
      u=(c-m)/beta
      e=np.exp(np.clip(u,-700,0)); s=e.sum(); w=e/s if s>0 else onehot
      w=w + floor*np.ones(L); w/=w.sum()
      B=_full_convolve(a[::-1], w)[n-1:2*n-1]
      W=float(np.dot(w,c))
      d=a*(W-B)
      d -= d.mean() # d_z sum already but numerical
      dn=np.linalg.norm(d)
      if dn < 1e-18: break
      # scale d so max relative step ~eta*
      maxd=np.max(np.abs(d))
      if maxd>1e-300:
          scale=min(1.0, (0.5*np.max(a))/maxd)
          d*=scale
      alpha=1.0; improved=False
      for _ in range(25):
          zt=z+alpha*d
          at=_softmax_centered(zt)
          sc=_score(at)
          if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
             best=a? actually # set a=at, best=...
             improved=True; break
          alpha*=0.5
          if alpha<1e-6: break
      if improved:
         a=at; best=a.copy(); best_sc=sc; eta=min(1.5, eta*1.05); stall=0
         _try_update(a,best_sc,'grad')
      else:
         stall+=1
         if stall>20: break
         # random kick
         if stall%5==0:
            z += 0.001*np.random.standard_normal(n)? Need rng. Pass rng.
```

Now, `_resample` and `_perturb`.

`_resample`:
```
def _resample(a,n_new):
   n=len(a)
   if n==n_new: return a.copy()
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n_new)
   a_new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,np.maximum(a,0))
   return _normalize(a_new)
```
Interpolation smooths high freq; maybe not ideal. Could use spectral upsampling by FFT: pad? But if we want same shape on finer grid, linear okay. For n=32768, could use nearest doubling (repeat) score same? Linear may initially worse. We can optionally average pairs? For upsample by factor 2, let new[2i]=new[2i+1]=a[i]/2 keeps score same; then optimize. This preserves high freq coarse. Better than interpolation? For factor r, block replication keeps score same but not smooth. We can use block replication for n_new multiple. Implement `_upreplicate(a,n_new)` if divisible: `np.repeat(a, r)/r`. If n_new not multiple, interpolate.
For n=32768 from 16384, r=2: repeat each coeff twice and divide by 2. Score? Let's verify a' length2n, a'_{2i}=a_i/2,a'_{2i+1}=a_i/2. Sum same. Conv at corresponding lag: each original pair contributes r? c'≈(1/2)c? Score same. Good. Use this as initial for larger n; optimizer can insert alternate variation. Better than interpolation. For downsampling? not needed. For resample to non-multiple, average.

Implement `_resample_exact(a,n_new)`:
```
r = n_new // n if n_new % n ==0:
   if r>=1: return normalize(np.repeat(a,r)/r)
else: interpolate
```
For n_new smaller, r=0 maybe use average blocks or interp.

`_perturb`:
```
def _perturb(a, sigma, rng):
   z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)) + sigma*rng.standard_normal(len(a))
   return _softmax_centered(z)
```

Pseudo propose:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    global _PROPOSE_START,_BEST_SEQ,_BEST_SCORE,_CUR_N,_CUR_BETA
    t0=time.monotonic(); _PROPOSE_START=t0
    budget=max(10.0,min(float(budget_s),1800.0))
    deadline=t0 + budget - 30.0
    rng=np.random.default_rng((seed % (2**32)) + 1)
    _BEST_SEQ=None; _BEST_SCORE=np.inf
    parent=_load_parent()
    if parent is not None:
       _try_update(parent, None, 'parent')
    else:
       init=_make_profile(16384,'asym', rng)
       _try_update(init,None,'init')
    if _BEST_SEQ is None: _try_update(_make_profile(16384,'uniform'),None,'fallback')
    # maybe quick active to improve before L-BFGS? Not necessary.
    phase1_deadline = min(t0 + 500.0, deadline - 900.0)
    if phase1_deadline > t0 + 30:
       a=_run_lbfgs_seq(_BEST_SEQ, phase1_deadline, 0.0, aggressive=True, label='main')
    phase2_deadline = min(phase1_deadline + 350.0, deadline - 650.0)
    if phase2_deadline > t0 + 360:
       a=sc=_active_flatten_loop(_BEST_SEQ, phase2_deadline, 0.0, max_iters=100, m_rows=180)
    phase3_deadline = min(phase2_deadline + 350.0, deadline - 400.0)
    if phase3_deadline > phase2_deadline + 30:
       a=_run_lbfgs_seq(_BEST_SEQ, phase3_deadline, 0.0, aggressive=True, label='main2')
    phase4_deadline = min(phase3_deadline + 300.0, deadline - 200.0)
    if phase4_deadline > phase3_deadline + 30:
       _active_flatten_loop(_BEST_SEQ, phase4_deadline, 0.0, max_iters=80, m_rows=180, eta_start=0.4)
    # gradient polish
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120:
       _gradient_polish(_BEST_SEQ, min(time.monotonic()+200.0, deadline-80), 0.0, rng=rng, max_steps=300, beta_frac=0.002)
    # final active tight
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
       _active_flatten_loop(_BEST_SEQ, min(time.monotonic()+100.0, deadline-30), 0.0, max_iters=100, m_rows=160, eta_start=0.5, eta_end=0.85)
    # upsample if time
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 180 and len(_BEST_SEQ) < 32768:
       a_big=_resample_exact(_BEST_SEQ, 32768)
       # maybe perturb slightly to allow alternation
       a_big=_perturb(a_big,0.01,rng)
       dl=min(time.monotonic()+120.0, deadline-60)
       _run_lbfgs_seq(a_big, dl, 0.0, aggressive=False, label='n32768')
       if time.monotonic() < deadline-40:
          _active_flatten_loop(_BEST_SEQ, min(time.monotonic()+40.0, deadline-20), 0.0, max_iters=30, m_rows=128)
    # random restart small
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 90:
       a_r=_perturb(_BEST_SEQ, 0.03, rng)
       dl=min(time.monotonic()+60.0, deadline-30)
       _run_lbfgs_seq(a_r, dl, 0.0, aggressive=False, label='restart')
    # final evaluator check
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None:
       final=_normalize(_BEST_SEQ)
       sc=_score(final)
       _try_update(final, sc, 'final')
       try:
          if time.monotonic() < t0 + budget - 10:
             ev=evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in _BEST_SEQ])
             print('evaluator score', ev)
       except Exception as e: print(...)
    if _BEST_SEQ is None: fallback
    return [float(x) for x in _normalize(_BEST_SEQ)]
```

Let's implement `_trim_sequence(arr)`:
```
def _trim_sequence(arr):
    a=_normalize(arr)
    n=len(a)
    if n<10: return a,_score(a)
    best=a; best_sc=_score(a)
    maxa=float(np.max(a))
    thresholds=[1e-12,1e-10,1e-8,1e-6,1e-4]
    for th_factor in thresholds:
       if maxa*th_factor > 0:
          idx=np.flatnonzero(a > maxa*th_factor)
          if idx.size==0: continue
          first=int(idx[0]); last=int(idx[-1])
          if first==0 and last==n-1: continue
          trimmed=a[first:last+1]
          if trimmed.size == n: continue
          sc=_score(trimmed)
          if sc < best_sc:
             best=trimmed; best_sc=sc
    return best,best_sc
```
If parent has zeros exactly, threshold 1e-12 trims. But if no zeros, thresholds may trim small edges. Be careful: threshold 1e-4*max may remove 15% edge mass; if score improves, great. Let's implement `_try_crop_search(a)`:
```

   best_arr=a; best_sc=_score(a); n=len(a)

Helper `_next_pow2`:
```
def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```
Actually for m=2, (1).bit_length=1? int(2)-1=1 bit_length1 =>2. ok. For m=1, returns1.

`_full_convolve`:
```
def _full_convolve(a,b):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64); b=...
    out=a.size+b.size-1
    if out<=0: return np.array([])
    N=_next_pow2(out)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
    res=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,N)
    return res[:out]
```
If a or b integer? convert.

`_score`:
```
def _score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if a.size==0: return np.inf
   a=np.maximum(a,0)
   s=float(a.sum())
   if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.inf
   c=_full_convolve(a,a)
   if c.size==0: return np.inf
   mx=float(np.max(c))
   if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx < 0: mx=0.0
   return float(2.0*a.size*mx/(s*s))
```
If max negative tiny due roundoff, set 0.

`_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(a):
  a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
  a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
  s=float(a.sum())
  if s<=1e-300: return a
  return a/s
```

def _crop_search(a, time_limit=10.0):
   arr=_normalize(a); n=arr.size; best=arr.copy(); best_sc=_score(arr)
   if n<200: return best,best_sc
   min_len=max(64, int(n*0.30))
   fr=[0.0,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.03,0.05,0.075,0.10,0.15,0.20]
   t0=time.monotonic()

def _run_lbfgs_seq(a_init, deadline, min_remaining=0.0, aggressive=True, label=''):
    a=_normalize(a_init)
    if a.size==0: return a
    local_best=a.copy(); local_sc=_score(local_best)
    _try_update(local_best, local_sc, label+'_start')
    n=a.size
    c=_full_convolve(a,a)
    maxc=float(np.max(c))
    if maxc <= 0 or not np.isfinite(maxc): return local_best
    sorted_c=np.sort(c)[::-1]
    idx=max(0, min(n*? maybe len c 0.8))
    gap=maxc - sorted_c[int(0.8*sorted_c.size)]
    base=max(gap*0.35, maxc*0.005)
    min_beta=max(maxc*(1e-8 if aggressive else 1e-6), 1e-300)
    ratios=...
    betas=[] ...
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z=z-np.mean(z)
    for idxb,beta in enumerate(betas):
        if time.monotonic() + min_remaining >= deadline: break
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 3: break
        remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 2: break
        _set_opt_state(n, beta)
        est=0.004 * max(1.0, n/16384.0)
        maxiter=int(min(4000, max(30, remaining/(est*20))))
        try:
            res=minimize(_opt_objective, z, jac=_opt_gradient, method='L-BFGS-B',
                         options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'ftol':1e-16, 'gtol':1e-14, 'maxcor':60, 'maxls':50})
        except Exception as e:
            print('lbfgs error', e); break
        a_new=_softmax_centered(res.x)
        sc=_score(a_new)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_sc - 1e-13:
            local_sc=sc; local_best=a_new.copy()
            z=res.x - np.mean(res.x)
            _try_update(local_best, local_sc, f'{label}_b{idxb}')
        # If beta very small and no improvement, maybe still update z? no.
    return local_best

```
def _active_flatten_loop(a_init, deadline, min_remaining, max_iters=100, m_rows=160, eta_start=0.05, eta_end=0.7, rng=None):
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(123)
    a=_normalize(a_init); best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(best)
    n=a.size; eta=eta_start; lam=1e-5; stall=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic()+min_remaining >= deadline: break
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2: break
       c=_full_convolve(best,best)
       cmax=float(np.max(c)); mean=float(np.mean(c))
       if cmax <= mean or cmax <=0: break
       target=cmax - eta*(cmax-mean)
       if target < 0.1*mean: target=0.1*mean
       res=_active_l_step(best, c, target, m_rows, lam, rng, best_sc)
       if res is None:
          stall += 1
          if stall <=2: lam*=4
          elif stall ==3: eta=max(0.02, eta*0.6); lam=1e-5
          else: break
          continue
       a_new, sc_new=res
       if sc_new < best_sc - 1e-13:
          best=a_new; best_sc=sc_new; stall=0
          eta=min(eta_end, eta*1.25)
          lam=max(1e-7, lam*0.7)
          _try_update(best, best_sc, f'active_{it}')
       else:
          stall += 1
    return best, best_sc
```
Need `_active_l_step` returns None or (a,sc).

`_active_l_step`:
```
def _active_l_step(a, c, target, m_rows, lam, rng, base_sc):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    if m_rows >= L: rows=np.arange(L)
    else:
       m_high=max(1,int(m_rows*0.6))
       m_rand=max(0,m_rows-m_high)
       if m_high >= L: high=np.arange(L)
       else: high=np.argpartition(-c, m_high-1)[:m_high]
       if m_rand > 0:
          rand=rng.choice(L, size=m_rand, replace=False)
          rows=np.unique(np.concatenate((high,rand)))
          if rows.size > m_rows: rows=rows[:m_rows]
       else: rows=high
    m=rows.size
    U=np.zeros((m,n), dtype=np.float64)
    for ii,k in enumerate(rows):
       k=int(k)
       lo=max(0, k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       length=hi-lo+1
       if length>0:
          U[ii,:length]=a[hi:lo-1:-1]
    e=c[rows] - target
    b=U.sum(axis=1)
    b_pos=b[b>0]
    b_mean=float(b_pos.mean()) if b_pos.size else 1.0
    if b_mean <= 1e-300: return None
    scale=np.clip(b / b_mean, 0.0, 1.0)
    r=-0.5 * e * scale
    # If r all zero return None
    if np.max(np.abs(r)) <= 1e-300: return None
    G=U @ U.T
    gmax=float(np.max(np.abs(G))) if G.size else 0.0
    reg = max(1e-12, (lam + 1e-6) * (gmax + 1e-300))
    A=G + reg*np.eye(m)
    AA=np.zeros((m+1,m+1), dtype=np.float64)
    AA[:m,:m]=A; AA[:m,m]=b; AA[m,:m]=b
    rr=np.zeros(m+1); rr[:m]=r
    try: sol=np.linalg.solve(AA, rr)
    except np.linalg.LinAlgError: sol=np.linalg.lstsq(AA, rr, rcond=None)[0]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(sol)): return None
    x=sol[:m]
    d=U.T @ x
    d = d - float(np.mean(d))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)) or np.linalg.norm(d) <= 1e-300: return None
    # Limit step
    a_max=float(np.max(a))
    d_max=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if a_max > 0 and d_max > 0:
       scale_step = min(1.0, (0.25 * a_max) / d_max)
       d *= scale_step
    neg = d < 0
    if np.any(neg):
       a_safe=np.maximum(a, 1e-10*a_max)
       limit = 0.95 * float(np.min(a_safe[neg] / (-d[neg])))
       if np.isfinite(limit) and limit > 0:
          d *= min(1.0, limit)
    best_a=a; best_sc=base_sc
    for alpha in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1):
       a_try = a + alpha*d
       # small clip
       if np.any(a_try < 0): a_try=np.maximum(a_try,0)
       s=float(a_try.sum())
       if s <= 1e-300: continue
       a_try = a_try / s
       sc=_score(a_try)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
          return a_try, sc
    return None
```
If d direction wrong, no try negative. Could include negative small if no improvement:
```
for alpha in (0.1,0.05,0.02): a_try=a-alpha*d ...
```
Maybe sign consistent, but if target/r sign bug, negative could help. Add before return None.

Potential issue: `rows=np.unique(np.concatenate(...))` order arbitrary; fine. If rows size less than m_rows due duplicates, okay.

Now, `_gradient_polish`:
```
def _gradient_polish(a_init, deadline, min_remaining, rng, max_steps=300, beta_frac=0.003, floor=1e-6):
    a=_normalize(a_init); best=a.copy(); best_sc=_score(a); n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z=z-z.mean()
    eta=1.0; stall=0
    for it in range(max_steps):
       if time.monotonic()+min_remaining >= deadline: break
       if time.monotonic() > deadline-2: break
       c=_full_convolve(a,a); m=float(np.max(c))
       if m<=0: break
       beta=max(m*beta_frac, 1e-12)
       u=(c-m)/beta
       e=np.exp(np.clip(u,-700,0)); s=float(e.sum())
       if s>0: w=e/s
       else: w=np.zeros(L); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
       w = w + floor*np.ones(L); w/=w.sum()
       B=_full_convolve(a[::-1], w)[n-1:2*n-1]
       W=float(np.dot(w,c))
       d=a*(W-B)
       d -= d.mean()
       if np.linalg.norm(d) < 1e-18: break
       a_max=float(np.max(a)); d_max=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
       if d_max>0: d*=min(1.0, (0.1*a_max)/d_max)
       alpha=1.0; improved=False
       for _ in range(20):
          zt=z+alpha*d
          at=_softmax_centered(zt)
          sc=_score(at)
          if sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
             best=at.copy(); best_sc=sc; z=zt; improved=True; break
          alpha*=0.5
          if alpha < 1e-6: break
       if improved:
          stall=0; eta=min(1.5, eta*1.05); _try_update(best,best_sc,'grad')
       else:
          stall+=1
          if stall > 30: break
          if stall % 10 == 0:
             z = z + 0.002 * rng.standard_normal(n)
             z = z - z.mean()
             best? maybe recompute a? continue
    return best,best_sc
```
This can be expensive if no improvement and recomputes score 20 each step. Use line search 8. Limit steps.

Now, `_make_profile` fallback. Need kind 'asym' using rng.
```
def _make_profile(n, kind, rng=None):
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    if kind=='uniform': a=np.ones(n)
    elif kind=='tapered': a=np.sin(np.pi*t)**0.5 + 1e-6
    elif kind=='asym':
       a=0.4+0.6*np.sin(np.pi*t)**0.3
       ph1=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi); ph2=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       a += 0.15*np.cos(2*np.pi*t+ph1)+0.08*np.cos(4*np.pi*t+ph2)+0.05*np.sin(8*np.pi*t+ph1*1.3)
       a=np.maximum(a,1e-3)
    else: a=np.ones(n)
    return _normalize(a)
```
For n=16384, score maybe ~1.8.

`_resample_exact`:
```
def _resample_exact(a,n_new):
   a=_normalize(a); n=a.size
   if n==n_new: return a
   if n_new > n:
      if n_new % n == 0:
         r=n_new//n
         out=np.repeat(a, r, axis=0)
         out *= 1.0/float(r)
         return _normalize(out)
   # general interpolate
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n_new)
   return _normalize(np.interp(x_new,x_old,a))
```
If downsample (n_new<n), interpolation may smooth. Good.

`_perturb`:
```
def _perturb(a, sigma, rng):
   z=np.log(np.maximum(_normalize(a),1e-300)) + sigma*rng.standard_normal(len(a))
   return _softmax_centered(z)
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    global _PROPOSE_START, _BEST_SEQ, _BEST_SCORE, _CUR_N, _CUR_BETA
    t0=time.monotonic(); _PROPOSE_START=t0
    budget=min(1800.0, max(60.0, float(1800 if budget_s is None else budget_s)))
    deadline=t0 + max(10.0, budget - 30.0)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2**32) + 1)
    _BEST_SEQ=None; _BEST_SCORE=np.inf
    parent=_load_parent()
    if parent is not None:
        _try_update(parent, None, 'parent')
    else:
        _try_update(_make_profile(16384,'asym',rng), None, 'init')
    if _BEST_SEQ is None:
        _try_update(_make_profile(8192,'uniform'), None, 'fallback')
    # crop parent
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None and deadline - t0 > 20:
        c_seq,c_sc=_crop_search(_BEST_SEQ, time_limit=8.0)
        _try_update(c_seq, c_sc, 'crop1')
        # if improved significantly and time, second crop
        if _BEST_SEQ is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 200:
            c_seq,c_sc=_crop_search(_BEST_SEQ, time_limit=5.0)
            _try_update(c_seq,c_sc,'crop2')
    # Phase 1 L-BFGS
    phase1 = min(t0 + 450.0, deadline - 900.0)
    if phase1 > time.monotonic() + 30:
        _run_lbfgs_seq(_BEST_SEQ, phase1, 0.0, True, 'main')
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 600:
            c_seq,c_sc=_crop_search(_BEST_SEQ, time_limit=8.0)
            _try_update(c_seq,c_sc,'crop_after_main')
    # Phase active
    phase2 = min(phase1 + 350.0, deadline - 650.0)
    if phase2 > time.monotonic() + 30:
        seq,sc = _active_flatten_loop(_BEST_SEQ, phase2, 0.0, max_iters=120, m_rows=180, eta_start=0.05, eta_end=0.7, rng=rng)
        _try_update(seq,sc,'active1')
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 500:
            c_seq,c_sc=_crop_search(_BEST_SEQ, time_limit=8.0); _try_update(c_seq,c_sc,'crop_active1')
    # Phase 2 L-BFGS
    phase3 = min(phase2 + 300.0, deadline - 400.0)
    if phase3 > time.monotonic() + 20:
        _run_lbfgs_seq(_BEST_SEQ, phase3, 0.0, True, 'main2')
        c_seq,c_sc=_crop_search(_BEST_SEQ, time_limit=6.0); _try_update(c_seq,c_sc,'crop_main2')
    # active tight
    phase4 = min(phase3 + 250.0, deadline - 200.0)
    if phase4 > time.monotonic() + 20:
        seq,sc=_active_flatten_loop(_BEST_SEQ, phase4, 0.0, max_iters=100, m_rows=160, eta_start=0.08, eta_end=0.8, rng=rng)
        _try_update(seq,sc,'active2')
    # gradient polish
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120:
        seq,sc=_gradient_polish(_BEST_SEQ, min(time.monotonic()+180.0, deadline-80), 0.0, rng, max_steps=250, beta_frac=0.002)
        _try_update(seq,sc,'grad')
    # final active very tight
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 80:
        seq,sc=_active_flatten_loop(_BEST_SEQ, min(time.monotonic()+80.0, deadline-30), 0.0, max_iters=80, m_rows=140, eta_start=0.1, eta_end=0.85, rng=rng)
        _try_update(seq,sc,'active_final')
    # upsample if shorter than 16384 or 32768
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 180 and len(_BEST_SEQ) < 32768:
        n_next = 32768 if len(_BEST_SEQ) < 16384 else 32768
        up=_resample_exact(_BEST_SEQ, n_next)
        up=_perturb(up, 0.01, rng)
        dl=min(time.monotonic()+120.0, deadline-60.0)
        _run_lbfgs_seq(up, dl, 0.0, False, 'up')
        if time.monotonic() < deadline-40:
            seq,sc=_active_flatten_loop(_BEST_SEQ, min(time.monotonic()+40.0, deadline-20), 0.0, max_iters=30, m_rows=120, eta_start=0.08, rng=rng)
            _try_update(seq,sc,'up_active')
        c_seq,c_sc=_crop_search(_BEST_SEQ, time_limit=4.0); _try_update(c_seq,c_sc,'crop_up')
    # restarts
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 90:
        rr=_perturb(_BEST_SEQ, 0.02, rng)
        dl=min(time.monotonic()+60.0, deadline-40.0)
        _run_lbfgs_seq(rr, dl, 0.0, False, 'restart')
        if time.monotonic() < deadline-30:
            seq,sc=_active_flatten_loop(_BEST_SEQ, min(time.monotonic()+20.0, deadline-15), 0.0, max_iters=20, m_rows=120, eta_start=0.1, rng=rng)
            _try_update(seq,sc,'restart_active')
    # final crop and trim
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None:
       c_seq,c_sc=_crop_search(_BEST_SEQ, time_limit=8.0); _try_update(c_seq,c_sc,'crop_final')
       # maybe small L-BFGS if crop changed length
       if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30:
           _run_lbfgs_seq(_BEST_SEQ, deadline-3, 0.0, True, 'final')
           c_seq,c_sc=_crop_search(_BEST_SEQ, time_limit=3.0); _try_update(c_seq,c_sc,'crop_final2')
    # final normalize
    final_seq = _normalize(_BEST_SEQ) if _BEST_SEQ is not None else _make_profile(8192,'uniform')
    _try_update(final_seq, None, 'final_return')
    # evaluator optional
    try:
       if time.monotonic() < t0 + budget - 8 and _BEST_SEQ is not None:
           ev=evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in final_seq])
           print('FINAL evaluate_sequence:', ev)
    except Exception:
       pass
    final_seq=_normalize(_BEST_SEQ) if _BEST_SEQ is not None else _make_profile(8192,'uniform')
    print(f"Returning n={len(final_seq)}, internal_score={_score(final_seq):.10f}, time={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}")
    return [float(x) for x in final_seq]
```

One more idea: Since target score 1.5027, we can use a hard lower target in active loop: target_c = (best_sc - 0.002)/(2n). But setting target too low may not be feasible because many coefficients above. However active step selection top m; target = min(formula, (best_sc - 0.002)/(2n))? Actually if formula target is higher (less aggressive), we can choose target closer to current max: `target = min(target, desired_c)`? `desired_c = (best_sc - 0.001)/(2n)` is slightly below cmax. The formula `cmax - eta*(cmax-mean)` for eta=0.02 is much lower. We want not too low; choose the larger (less aggressive) of formula and desired? We want target not below cmax - small reduction. Use `target = cmax - max(desired_delta, eta*(cmax-mean))`? Actually larger target = less aggressive. Let `delta = max(eta*(cmax-mean), 0.002/(2*n)? no)`. To be close, set `delta = min(eta*(cmax-mean), max(current_score_target?))`. Hmm.

We want target = cmax - delta. If delta too large, over-step. Let delta = min(eta*(cmax-mean), 0.01*(cmax-mean))? But we want start small. Maybe set `delta = eta * (cmax - mean)` with eta start 0.02. That's fine. Add also a hard maximum delta score reduction: `delta = min(delta, 0.005/(2*n))`? 0.005/(2n) is score reduction 0.0025. For current, 0.0025/n? Wait score delta 0.0025 corresponds c delta =0.0025/(2n)=1.25e-3/n. cmax-mean≈0.25/n, eta=0.005. So min would make eta=0.005. Maybe too small. For score improve 0.003, c delta=1.5e-3/n. Hard max 0.005? okay. Let's not add hard max; line search handles.

But if eta grows to 0.5, target very low, direction may not improve; loop stalls and reduces eta after failures. Good.

Now, perhaps we should use `_active_flatten_loop` with `m_rows=220` for more constraints. Time maybe. Use 180. Good.

```python
"""
Search for a nonnegative step-function sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a)^2).

Strategy:
- Start from the parent sequence if available.
- Exploit length-free zero-padding: try aggressive contiguous cropping and
  keep shorter sequences if the objective improves.
- Main polish with L-BFGS on a smooth maximization of linear-convolution
  coefficients in log-softmax coordinates.
- A custom active-row linearized least-squares polishing step that directly
  tries to flatten selected high/low convolution coefficients.
- A mirror-descent / softmax gradient polish with floor weights.
- Limited larger-n / perturbation exploration, always returning the best
  sequence found by internal FFT-based scoring.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_PROPOSE_START = 0.0
_CUR_N = 0
_CUR_BETA = 1.0
_BEST_SEQ = None
_BEST_SCORE = np.inf


def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def _full_convolve(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    out_len = a.size + b.size - 1
    if out_len <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    nfft = _next_pow2(out_len)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, nfft)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, nfft)[:out_len]


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return a
    return a / s


def _score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    c = _full_convolve(a, a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx < 0.0:
        mx = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * mx / (s * s))


def _softmax_centered(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = z.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    m = float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z - m, -700.0, 700.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _set_opt_state(n, beta):
    global _CUR_N, _CUR_BETA
    _CUR_N = int(n)
    _CUR_BETA = float(beta)


def _opt_objective(z):
    n = _CUR_N
    beta = _CUR_BETA
    if n <= 0:
        return 0.0
    a = _softmax_centered(z)
    c = _full_convolve(a, a)
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return 1e100
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return 1e100
    if beta <= 1e-300:
        return float(2.0 * n * m)
    u = (c - m) / beta
    u = np.clip(u, -700.0, 0.0)
    s = float(np.exp(u).sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        logsum = 0.0
    else:
        logsum = float(np.log(s))
    val = m + beta * (logsum - np.log(float(L)))
    return float(2.0 * n * val)


def _opt_gradient(z):
    n = _CUR_N
    beta = _CUR_BETA
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = _softmax_centered(z)
    c = _full_convolve(a, a)
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if beta <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        u = (c - m) / beta
        u = np.clip(u, -700.0, 0.0)
        e = np.exp(u)
        s = float(e.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = e / s
    hw = _full_convolve(a[::-1], w)
    B = hw[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    if B.size < n:
        B = np.pad(B, (0, n - B.size), mode="constant")
    W = float(np.dot(w, c))
    grad = 4.0 * float(n) * a * (B - W)
    grad = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return grad


def _load_parent():
    try:
        raw = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if raw is None:
        return None
    arr = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return arr / s


def _try_update(seq, score_hint, label):
    global _BEST_SEQ, _BEST_SCORE
    if seq is None:
        return
    arr = _normalize(seq)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return
    # Trim exact / near-exact zero ends safely.
    if arr.size > 10:
        maxa = float(np.max(arr))
        if maxa > 0.0:
            idx = np.flatnonzero(arr > max(1e-15 * maxa, 1e-300))
            if idx.size > 0:
                first = int(idx[0])
                last = int(idx[-1])
                if first > 0 or last < arr.size - 1:
                    arr = arr[first:last + 1]
    sc = _score(arr)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    if sc < _BEST_SCORE - 1e-14:
        _BEST_SEQ = arr.copy()
        _BEST_SCORE = float(sc)
        try:
            print(f"NEW BEST {sc:.10f} n={arr.size} {label} t={time.monotonic() - _PROPOSE_START:.1f}s")
        except Exception:
            pass


def _make_profile(n, kind, rng=None):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    n = int(max(1, n))
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    if kind == "uniform":
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    elif kind == "tapered":
        a = np.sin(np.pi * t) ** 0.5 + 1e-6
    elif kind == "asym":
        a = 0.4 + 0.6 * (np.sin(np.pi * t) ** 0.3)
        ph1 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        ph2 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        a += 0.15 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t + ph1)
        a += 0.08 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * t + ph2)
        a += 0.05 * np.sin(8.0 * np.pi * t + 1.3 * ph1)
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-3)
    else:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return _normalize(a)


def _resample_exact(a, n_new):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    n_new = int(n_new)
    if n_new <= 0:
        return a
    if n == n_new:
        return a
    if n_new > n and n_new % n == 0:
        r = n_new // n
        out = np.repeat(a, r, axis=0)
        out *= 1.0 / float(r)
        return _normalize(out)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
    return _normalize(np.interp(x_new, x_old, a))


def _perturb(a, sigma, rng):
    a = _normalize(a)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    z = z + float(sigma) * rng.standard_normal(a.size)
    return _softmax_centered(z)


def _crop_search(a, time_limit=10.0):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    if n < 200:
        return best, best_sc
    min_len = max(64, int(0.30 * n))
    fr = [0.0, 0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.03, 0.05, 0.075, 0.10, 0.15, 0.20]
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    for lf in fr:
        for rf in fr:
            if time.monotonic() - t0 > float(time_limit):
                break
            l = int(n * lf)
            r = int(n * rf)
            if n - l - r < min_len:
                continue
            if l == 0 and r == 0:
                continue
            seg = a[l:n - r]
            sc = _score(seg)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                best = seg.copy()
                best_sc = float(sc)
    return best, best_sc


def _active_l_step(a, c, target, m_rows, lam, rng, base_sc):
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if n == 0 or L == 0:
        return None
    m_rows = int(min(max(1, m_rows), L))
    if m_rows >= L:
        rows = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        m_high = int(max(1, 0.6 * m_rows))
        m_rand = int(max(0, m_rows - m_high))
        if m_high >= L:
            high = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            high = np.argpartition(-c, m_high - 1)[:m_high].astype(np.int64)
        rows_list = [int(k) for k in high]
        seen = set(rows_list)
        if m_rand > 0:
            rand = rng.choice(L, size=m_rand, replace=False)
            for kk in rand:
                kk = int(kk)
                if len(rows_list) >= m_rows:
                    break
                if kk not in seen:
                    rows_list.append(kk)
                    seen.add(kk)
        rows = np.asarray(rows_list[:m_rows], dtype=np.int64)

    m = rows.size
    if m == 0:
        return None
    U = np.zeros((m, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for ii, k in enumerate(rows):
        k = int(k)
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        length = hi - lo + 1
        if length > 0:
            U[ii, :length] = a[hi:lo - 1:-1]

    e = c[rows] - float(target)
    b = U.sum(axis=1)
    b_pos = b[b > 0]
    b_mean = float(b_pos.mean()) if b_pos.size > 0 else 1.0
    if b_mean <= 1e-300:
        return None
    scale = np.clip(b / b_mean, 0.0, 1.0)
    r = -0.5 * e * scale
    if not np.any(np.abs(r) > 1e-300):
        return None

    G = U @ U.T
    gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(G))) if G.size else 0.0
    reg = max(1e-12, (float(lam) + 1e-6) * (gmax + 1e-300))
    A = G + reg * np.eye(m, dtype=np.float64)

    AA = np.zeros((m + 1, m + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    AA[:m, :m] = A
    AA[:m, m] = b
    AA[m, :m] = b

    rr = np.zeros(m + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    rr[:m] = r
    try:
        sol = np.linalg.solve(AA, rr)
    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:
        sol = np.linalg.lstsq(AA, rr, rcond=None)[0]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(sol)):
        return None

    x = sol[:m]
    d = U.T @ x
    d = d - float(np.mean(d))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)) or float(np.linalg.norm(d)) <= 1e-300:
        return None

    a_max = float(np.max(a))
    d_max = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if a_max > 0.0 and d_max > 0.0:
        d *= min(1.0, (0.25 * a_max) / d_max)

    neg = d < 0.0
    if np.any(neg):
        a_safe = np.maximum(a, 1e-10 * a_max)
        limit = 0.95 * float(np.min(a_safe[neg] / (-d[neg])))
        if np.isfinite(limit) and limit > 0.0:
            d *= min(1.0, limit)

    best_a = a
    best_sc = float(base_sc)
    for alpha in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1):
        a_try = a + alpha * d
        if np.any(a_try < 0.0):
            a_try = np.maximum(a_try, 0.0)
        s = float(a_try.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            continue
        a_try = a_try / s
        sc = _score(a_try)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
            return a_try, sc

    # Very small reverse step, mostly as a safety net.
    for alpha in (0.05, 0.02, 0.01):
        a_try = a - alpha * d
        if np.any(a_try < 0.0):
            a_try = np.maximum(a_try, 0.0)
        s = float(a_try.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            continue
        a_try = a_try / s
        sc = _score(a_try)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
            return a_try, sc

    return None


def _active_flatten_loop(a_init, deadline, min_remaining, max_iters=100,
                         m_rows=160, eta_start=0.02, eta_end=0.8, rng=None):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
    a = _normalize(a_init)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    eta = float(eta_start)
    lam = 1e-5
    stall = 0
    for it in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() + float(min_remaining) >= deadline:
            break
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2:
            break
        c = _full_convolve(best, best)
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        mean = float(np.mean(c))
        if cmax <= 0.0 or cmax <= mean:
            break
        target = cmax - eta * (cmax - mean)
        if target < 0.1 * mean:
            target = 0.1 * mean
        res = _active_l_step(best, c, target, m_rows, lam, rng, best_sc)
        if res is None:
            stall += 1
            if stall <= 2:
                lam *= 4.0
            elif stall == 3:
                eta = max(0.005, eta * 0.6)
                lam = 1e-5
            else:
                break
            continue
        a_new, sc_new = res
        if sc_new < best_sc - 1e-13:
            best = a_new
            best_sc = float(sc_new)
            stall = 0
            eta = min(float(eta_end), eta * 1.2)
            lam = max(1e-7, lam * 0.7)
            _try_update(best, best_sc, f"active_{it}")
        else:
            stall += 1
    return best, best_sc


def _run_lbfgs_seq(a_init, deadline, min_remaining=0.0, aggressive=True, label=""):
    a = _normalize(a_init)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    local_best = a.copy()
    local_sc = _score(local_best)
    _try_update(local_best, local_sc, label + "_start")
    n = a.size
    c = _full_convolve(a, a)
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc <= 0.0:
        return local_best
    sorted_c = np.sort(c)[::-1]
    idx = min(int(0.9 * sorted_c.size), sorted_c.size - 1)
    gap = maxc - float(sorted_c[idx])
    base = max(gap * 0.35, maxc * 0.005)
    min_beta = max(maxc * (1e-8 if aggressive else 1e-6), 1e-300)
    ratios = [
        1.0, 0.6, 0.4, 0.25, 0.15, 0.09, 0.055, 0.033, 0.02, 0.012,
        0.007, 0.004, 0.0024, 0.0014, 0.0008, 0.0005, 0.0003, 0.0002,
        0.00012, 0.00008, 0.00005
    ]
    betas = []
    for rr in ratios:
        b = base * rr
        if b < min_beta:
            b = min_beta
        if len(betas) == 0 or b < betas[-1] * 0.95:
            betas.append(float(b))

    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    stall = 0

    for bi, beta in enumerate(betas):
        if time.monotonic() + float(min_remaining) >= deadline:
            break
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 3:
            break
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 2.0:
            break
        _set_opt_state(n, beta)
        scale_n = max(1.0, float(n) / 16384.0)
        est = 0.02 * scale_n
        maxiter = int(min(1500, max(20, remaining / (est * 10.0))))
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _opt_objective,
                z,
                jac=_opt_gradient,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "ftol": 1e-16,
                    "gtol": 1e-14,
                    "maxcor": 60,
                    "maxls": 50,
                }
            )
        except Exception as ex:
            print("L-BFGS exception:", ex)
            break

        a_new = _softmax_centered(res.x)
        sc_new = _score(a_new)
        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < local_sc - 1e-13:
            local_sc = float(sc_new)
            local_best = a_new.copy()
            z = res.x - float(np.mean(res.x))
            _try_update(local_best, local_sc, f"{label}_b{bi}")
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 3:
                break
    return local_best


def _gradient_polish(a_init, deadline, min_remaining, rng, max_steps=250,
                     beta_frac=0.002, floor=1e-6):
    a = _normalize(a_init)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a, np.inf
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score(a)
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    eta = 1.0
    stall = 0

    for it in range(int(max_steps)):
        if time.monotonic() + float(min_remaining) >= deadline:
            break
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2:
            break
        c = _full_convolve(a, a)
        m = float(np.max(c))
        if m <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(m):
            break
        beta = max(m * float(beta_frac), 1e-12)
        u = (c - m) / beta
        e = np.exp(np.clip(u, -700.0, 0.0))
        s = float(e.sum())
        if s > 1e-300 and np.isfinite(s):
            w = e / s
        else:
            w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        w = w + float(floor) * np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w /= w.sum()

        B = _full_convolve(a[::-1], w)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
        if B.size < n:
            B = np.pad(B, (0, n - B.size), mode="constant")
        W = float(np.dot(w, c))
        d = a * (W - B)
        d = d - float(np.mean(d))
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)) or float(np.linalg.norm(d)) < 1e-18:
            break

        a_max = float(np.max(a))
        d_max = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
        if d_max > 0.0:
            d *= min(1.0, (0.1 * a_max) / d_max)

        alpha = 1.0
        improved = False
        for _ in range(12):
            zt = z + alpha * d
            at = _softmax_centered(zt)
            sc = _score(at)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                best = at.copy()
                best_sc = float(sc)
                z = zt
                improved = True
                break
            alpha *= 0.5
            if alpha < 1e-6:
                break

        if improved:
            stall = 0
            eta = min(1.5, eta * 1.05)
            _try_update(best, best_sc, f"grad_{it}")
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 25:
                break
            if stall % 8 == 0:
                z = z + 0.002 * rng.standard_normal(n)
                z = z - float(np.mean(z))
                a = _softmax_centered(z)
    return best, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800):
    global _PROPOSE_START, _CUR_N, _CUR_BETA, _BEST_SEQ, _BEST_SCORE

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    _PROPOSE_START = t0
    try:
        budget = min(1800.0, max(60.0, float(budget_s)))
    except Exception:
        budget = 1800.0
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, budget - 30.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng((int(seed) % (2**32)) + 1)

    _BEST_SEQ = None
    _BEST_SCORE = np.inf

    parent = _load_parent()
    if parent is not None:
        _try_update(parent, None, "parent")
    else:
        _try_update(_make_profile(16384, "asym", rng), None, "init")

    if _BEST_SEQ is None:
        _try_update(_make_profile(8192, "uniform"), None, "fallback")

    if _BEST_SEQ is not None and deadline - t0 > 25.0:
        cseq, csc = _crop_search(_BEST_SEQ, time_limit=8.0)
        _try_update(cseq, csc, "crop1")
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 250.0:
            cseq, csc = _crop_search(_BEST_SEQ, time_limit=5.0)
            _try_update(cseq, csc, "crop2")

    phase1 = min(t0 + 450.0, deadline - 900.0)
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None and phase1 > time.monotonic() + 30.0:
        _run_lbfgs_seq(_BEST_SEQ, phase1, 0.0, True, "main")
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 650.0:
            cseq, csc = _crop_search(_BEST_SEQ, time_limit=8.0)
            _try_update(cseq, csc, "crop_after_main")

    phase2 = min(phase1 + 350.0, deadline - 650.0)
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None and phase2 > time.monotonic() + 30.0:
        seq, sc = _active_flatten_loop(
            _BEST_SEQ, phase2, 0.0,
            max_iters=120, m_rows=180,
            eta_start=0.02, eta_end=0.7, rng=rng
        )
        _try_update(seq, sc, "active1")
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 500.0:
            cseq, csc = _crop_search(_BEST_SEQ, time_limit=8.0)
            _try_update(cseq, csc, "crop_active1")

    phase3 = min(phase2 + 300.0, deadline - 400.0)
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None and phase3 > time.monotonic() + 20.0:
        _run_lbfgs_seq(_BEST_SEQ, phase3, 0.0, True, "main2")
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 300.0:
            cseq, csc = _crop_search(_BEST_SEQ, time_limit=6.0)
            _try_update(cseq, csc, "crop_main2")

    phase4 = min(phase3 + 250.0, deadline - 200.0)
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None and phase4 > time.monotonic() + 20.0:
        seq, sc = _active_flatten_loop(
            _BEST_SEQ, phase4, 0.0,
            max_iters=100, m_rows=160,
            eta_start=0.03, eta_end=0.8, rng=rng
        )
        _try_update(seq, sc, "active2")

    if _BEST_SEQ is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 130.0:
        seq, sc = _gradient_polish(
            _BEST_SEQ, min(time.monotonic() + 180.0, deadline - 80.0), 0.0,
            rng, max_steps=220, beta_frac=0.002, floor=1e-6
        )
        _try_update(seq, sc, "grad")
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 100.0:
            cseq, csc = _crop_search(_BEST_SEQ, time_limit=5.0)
            _try_update(cseq, csc, "crop_grad")

    if _BEST_SEQ is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 90.0:
        seq, sc = _active_flatten_loop(
            _BEST_SEQ, min(time.monotonic() + 80.0, deadline - 30.0), 0.0,
            max_iters=80, m_rows=140,
            eta_start=0.02, eta_end=0.9, rng=rng
        )
        _try_update(seq, sc, "active_final")

    # Optional larger-n exploration. Revert if it does not earn its runtime
    # and length penalty.
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 200.0 and len(_BEST_SEQ) < 32768:
        saved_seq = _BEST_SEQ.copy()
        saved_sc = float(_BEST_SCORE)
        up = _resample_exact(_BEST_SEQ, 32768)
        up = _perturb(up, 0.01, rng)
        dline = min(time.monotonic() + 120.0, deadline - 70.0)
        _run_lbfgs_seq(up, dline, 0.0, False, "up")
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 50.0:
            seq, sc = _active_flatten_loop(
                _BEST_SEQ, min(time.monotonic() + 40.0, deadline - 30.0), 0.0,
                max_iters=30, m_rows=120, eta_start=0.03, rng=rng
            )
            _try_update(seq, sc, "up_active")
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
            cseq, csc = _crop_search(_BEST_SEQ, time_limit=4.0)
            _try_update(cseq, csc, "crop_up")
        if _BEST_SCORE > saved_sc - 2e-5:
            _BEST_SEQ = saved_seq
            _BEST_SCORE = saved_sc
            _try_update(_BEST_SEQ, saved_sc, "revert_up")

    # Random restart on current best.
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 100.0:
        rr = _perturb(_BEST_SEQ, 0.02, rng)
        dline = min(time.monotonic() + 60.0, deadline - 40.0)
        _run_lbfgs_seq(rr, dline, 0.0, False, "restart")
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
            seq, sc = _active_flatten_loop(
                _BEST_SEQ, min(time.monotonic() + 20.0, deadline - 15.0), 0.0,
                max_iters=20, m_rows=120, eta_start=0.03, rng=rng
            )
            _try_update(seq, sc, "restart_active")

    # Final crop and small finishing L-BFGS.
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None:
        cseq, csc = _crop_search(_BEST_SEQ, time_limit=8.0)
        _try_update(cseq, csc, "crop_final")
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
            _run_lbfgs_seq(_BEST_SEQ, deadline - 3.0, 0.0, True, "final")
            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
                cseq, csc = _crop_search(_BEST_SEQ, time_limit=3.0)
                _try_update(cseq, csc, "crop_final2")

    final_seq = _normalize(_BEST_SEQ) if _BEST_SEQ is not None else _make_profile(8192, "uniform")
    _try_update(final_seq, None, "final_return")

    try:
        if time.monotonic() < t0 + budget - 8.0 and _BEST_SEQ is not None:
            ev = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in final_seq])
            print("FINAL evaluate_sequence:", ev)
    except Exception:
        pass

    final_seq = _normalize(_BEST_SEQ) if _BEST_SEQ is not None else _make_profile(8192, "uniform")
    final_sc = _score(final_seq)
    print(f"Returning n={len(final_seq)}, internal_score={final_sc:.10f}, time={time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}s")
    return [float(x) for x in final_seq]
```
